59: function ensureSetup_() {
60:   if (SETUP_READY_) {
61:     return;
62:   }
63: 
64:   const ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
65: 
66:   if (!ss) {
67:     throw new Error(
68:       'Google 스프레드시트의 확장 프로그램 → Apps Script에서 실행하세요.'
69:     );
70:   }
71: 
72:   const setupCache = CacheService.getDocumentCache();
73:   const setupKey = 'JAYUMINTON_SETUP_V11_' + ss.getId();
74: 
75:   if (setupCache.get(setupKey) === '1') {
76:     SETUP_READY_ = true;
77:     return;
78:   }
79: 
80:   ensureMembersSheet_(ss);
81:   ensureCourtsSheet_(ss);
82:   ensureWaitSheet_(ss);

86:   trimLogs_();
87:   cleanupLegacyCourtData_();
88:   setupCache.put(setupKey, '1', 21600);
89:   SETUP_READY_ = true;
90: }

212: function withDocumentLock_(actionName, callback) {
213:   const lock = LockService.getDocumentLock();
214: 
215:   if (!lock.tryLock(15000)) {
216:     throw new Error(
217:       '다른 작업을 처리 중입니다. 잠시 후 다시 눌러주세요.'
218:     );
219:   }
220: 
221:   try {
222:     ensureSetup_();
223: 
224:     const result = callback();
225: 
226:     if (shouldLogSuccess_(actionName)) {
227:       logAction_(
228:         actionName,
229:         '정상 처리'
230:       );
231:     }
232: 
233:     return result;
234:   } catch (error) {
235:     logAction_(
236:       actionName,
237:       '오류: ' + String(error.message || error)
238:     );
239: 
240:     throw error;
241:   } finally {
242:     lock.releaseLock();
243:   }
244: }

246: function logAction_(action, detail) {
247:   try {
248:     const ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
249:     const sheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEET_LOGS);
250: 
251:     if (!sheet) {
252:       return;
253:     }
254: 
255:     sheet.appendRow([
256:       new Date(),
257:       String(action || ''),
258:       String(detail || '').slice(0, 300),
259:       Session.getActiveUser().getEmail() || ''
260:     ]);
261: 
262:     trimLogs_();
263:   } catch (error) {
264:     // 로그 실패가 실제 배정 작업을 막지 않게 함
265:   }
266: }

268: function shouldLogSuccess_(actionName) {
269:   return [
270:     '경기 종료 자동 순환',

273:     '수동 백업',

276:     '멤버 비밀번호 변경'
277:   ].indexOf(String(actionName)) >= 0;
278: }

495: function createBackupSnapshot_() {
496:   const ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
497: 
498:   [
499:     SHEET_MEMBERS,
500:     SHEET_COURTS,
501:     SHEET_WAIT,
502:     SHEET_SETTINGS
503:   ].forEach(function(sourceName) {
504:     const source = ss.getSheetByName(sourceName);
505:     const backupName =
506:       BACKUP_PREFIX + sourceName;
507: 
508:     const existing =
509:       ss.getSheetByName(backupName);
510: 
511:     if (existing) {
512:       ss.deleteSheet(existing);
513:     }
514: 
515:     const copy = source.copyTo(ss);
516:     copy.setName(backupName);
517:     copy.hideSheet();
518:   });
519: 
520:   setSetting_(
521:     'LAST_BACKUP_AT',
522:     new Date().toISOString()
523:   );
524: }

632: 
633:   if (!oldSheet) {
634:     setSetting_('LEGACY_MIGRATED', '1');
635:     return;
636:   }
637: 
638:   const values =
639:     oldSheet.getRange('A:B').getDisplayValues();
640: 
641:   const legacy = {};
642: 
643:   for (let i = 1; i < values.length; i++) {
644:     legacy[String(values[i][0])] = values[i][1];
645:   }
646: 
647:   const currentMembers = readMembers_();
648: 
649:   if (
650:     currentMembers.length === 0 &&
651:     legacy.MEMBERS
652:   ) {
653:     try {
654:       const members = JSON.parse(legacy.MEMBERS);
655: 
656:       members.forEach(function(member) {
657:         if (!member || !member.id || !member.name) {
658:           return;
659:         }
660: 
661:         appendMember_({
662:           id: String(member.id),
663:           name: String(member.name),
664:           gender:
665:             member.gender === 'female'
666:               ? 'female'
667:               : 'male',
668:           games: Number(member.games) || 0,
669:           status: normalizeStatus_(member.status),
670:           createdAt: new Date().toISOString(),
671:           grade: String(member.grade || ''),
672:           experience: String(member.experience || '')
673:         });
---
676:   }
677: 
678:   if (legacy.COURTS) {
679:     try {
680:       const courts = JSON.parse(legacy.COURTS);
681:       writeCourts_(courts);
682:     } catch (error) {}
683:   }
684: 
685:   if (legacy.WAIT_GROUPS) {
686:     try {
687:       const waitGroups =
688:         JSON.parse(legacy.WAIT_GROUPS);
689:       writeWaitGroups_(waitGroups);
690:     } catch (error) {}
691:   }
692: 
693:   if (legacy.MEMBER_PASSWORD) {
694:     setSetting_(
695:       'MEMBER_PASSWORD',
696:       String(legacy.MEMBER_PASSWORD)
697:     );
698:   }
699: 
700:   if (legacy.MEMBER_PASSWORD_VERSION) {
701:     setSetting_(
702:       'MEMBER_PASSWORD_VERSION',
703:       String(legacy.MEMBER_PASSWORD_VERSION)
704:     );
705:   }
706: 
707:   setSetting_('LEGACY_MIGRATED', '1');
708: }
709: 
710: function normalizeStatus_(status) {
711:   if (
712:     ['before', 'active', 'rest', 'away', 'playing', 'waiting']
713:       .indexOf(status) >= 0
714:   ) {
715:     return status;
716:   }
717: 
718:   if (
719:     status === 'unassigned'
720:   ) {
721:     return 'active';
722:   }
723: 
724:   return 'active';
725: }

727: function adminLogin(pin) {
728:   return String(pin == null ? '' : pin).trim() ===
729:     String(ADMIN_PIN).trim();
730: }

732: function adminSessionToken_() {
733:   const spreadsheet =
734:     SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
735: 
736:   const source = [
737:     String(ADMIN_PIN).trim(),
738:     spreadsheet ? spreadsheet.getId() : '',
739:     'JAYUMINTON_ADMIN_SESSION_V1'
740:   ].join('|');
741: 
742:   return Utilities
743:     .base64EncodeWebSafe(
744:       Utilities.computeDigest(
745:         Utilities.DigestAlgorithm.SHA_256,
746:         source
747:       )
748:     )
749:     .replace(/=+$/g, '');
750: }

887: function getPublicState() {
888:   ensureSetup_();
889: 
890:   const members = readMembers_();
891:   const courts = readCourts_();
892:   const waitGroups = readWaitGroups_();
893:   const courtStartedAt = readCourtStartedAt_();
894: 
895:   const validIds = {};
896:   members.forEach(function(member) {
897:     validIds[member.id] = true;
898:   });
899: 
900:   Object.keys(courts).forEach(function(key) {
901:     courts[key] =
902:       (courts[key] || []).filter(function(id) {
903:         return validIds[id];
904:       });
905:   });
906: 
907:   const cleanWait =
908:     waitGroups.map(function(group) {
909:       return (group || []).filter(function(id) {
910:         return validIds[id];
911:       });
912:     });
913: 
914:   return {
915:     members: members,
916:     courts: courts,
917:     waitGroups: cleanWait,
918:     courtStartedAt: courtStartedAt,
919:     updatedAt:
920:       getSetting_('UPDATED_AT') ||
921:       new Date().toISOString(),
922:     maxMembers: MAX_MEMBERS
923:   };
924: }

1065: function addMemberUnlocked_(pin, name, gender, grade, experience, isNew) {
1066:   auth_(pin);
1067: 
1068:   name = String(name == null ? '' : name).trim();
1069:   gender = String(gender == null ? '' : gender).trim();
1070:   grade = String(grade == null ? '' : grade).trim();
1071:   experience = String(experience == null ? '' : experience).trim();
1072:   isNew = isNew === true || String(isNew || '').toLowerCase() === 'true';
1073: 
1074:   if (!name || name.length > 20) {
1075:     throw new Error(
1076:       '이름 또는 닉네임은 1자 이상 20자 이하로 입력하세요.'
1077:     );
1078:   }
1079: 
1080:   if (
1081:     ['male', 'female'].indexOf(gender) < 0
1082:   ) {
1083:     throw new Error('성별을 선택하세요.');
1084:   }
1085: 
1086:   if (grade.length > 12) {
1087:     throw new Error('급수는 12자 이내로 입력하세요.');
1088:   }
1089: 
1090:   if (experience.length > 20) {
1091:     throw new Error('구력은 20자 이내로 입력하세요.');
1092:   }
1093: 
1094:   const members = readMembers_();
1095: 
1096:   if (members.length >= MAX_MEMBERS) {
1097:     throw new Error('멤버는 최대 500명입니다.');
1098:   }
1099: 
1100:   const member = {
1101:     id: Utilities.getUuid(),
1102:     name: name,
1103:     gender: gender,
1104:     games: 0,
1105:     status: 'active',
1106:     createdAt: new Date().toISOString(),
1107:     grade: grade,
1108:     experience: experience,
1109:     level: grade,
1110:     career: experience,
1111:     isNew: isNew
1112:   };
1113: 
1114:   appendMember_(member);
1115:   members.push(member);
1116:   touch_();
1117: 
1118:   /*
1119:    * 멤버 한 명 저장 뒤 코트·대기조 전체를 다시 읽으면 체감 지연이 커진다.
1120:    * 현재 웹/앱 화면은 result.member를 STATE에 합칠 수 있으므로 가벼운 응답만 반환한다.
1121:    */
1122:   return {
1123:     ok: true,
1124:     member: member,
1125:     updatedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
1126:     maxMembers: MAX_MEMBERS
1127:   };
1128: }

1124:     member: member,
1125:     updatedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
1126:     maxMembers: MAX_MEMBERS
1127:   };

1130: function setMemberStatusUnlocked_(pin, ids, status) {
1131:   auth_(pin);
1132: 
1133:   ids = normalizeIds_(ids);
1134: 
1135:   if (!ids.length) {
1136:     throw new Error('멤버를 선택하세요.');
1137:   }
1138: 
1139:   if (
1140:     ['before', 'active', 'rest', 'away']
1141:       .indexOf(status) < 0
1142:   ) {
1143:     throw new Error('잘못된 상태입니다.');
1144:   }
1145: 
1146:   removeEverywhere_(ids);
1147: 
1148:   const members = readMembers_();
1149: 
1150:   members.forEach(function(member) {
1151:     if (ids.indexOf(member.id) >= 0) {
1152:       member.status = status;
1153:     }
1154:   });
1155: 
1156:   writeMembers_(members);
1157:   touch_();
1158: 
1159:   return getPublicState();
1160: }

1210: function autoFillCourtUnlocked_(pin, courtNo, ids) {

1214:   ids = normalizeIds_(ids);
1215:

1250:     }
1251:     if (member.gender === 'female') femaleCount++;
1252:     else maleCount++;
1253:   });
1254: 
1255:   if (

1257:       maleCount === 4 ||
1258:       femaleCount === 4 ||

1260:     )
1261:   ) {
---
1192:     );
1193:   }
1194: 
1195:   removeEverywhere_(ids);
1196: 
1197:   const refreshedCourts = readCourts_();

1199:   refreshedCourts[courtNo] =
1200:     (refreshedCourts[courtNo] || []).concat(ids);
1201: 
1202:   markCourtStartedIfFull_(refreshedCourts, startedAt, courtNo);
1203:   writeCourts_(refreshedCourts, startedAt);

1300:     GROUP_SIZE

1321: function autoFillWaitGroupUnlocked_(
1322:   pin,

1324:   ids

1333:     groupIndex >= WAIT_GROUP_COUNT

1336:   }
1337:

1443:   const courts = readCourts_();
1444:   const startedAt = readCourtStartedAt_();
1445:   const waitGroups = readWaitGroups_();
1446:   const finished = courts[courtNo] || [];
1447: 
1448:   if (finished.length !== GROUP_SIZE) {
1449:     throw new Error(
1450:       '4명이 모두 배정된 코트만 경기 종료할 수 있습니다.'
1451:     );
1452:   }
1453: 
1454:   const members = readMembers_();
1455: 
1456:   members.forEach(function(member) {
1457:     if (finished.indexOf(member.id) >= 0) {
1458:       member.games =
1459:         (Number(member.games) || 0) + 1;
1460:       member.status = 'active';
1461:     }
1462:   });
1463: 
1464:   const waitOne = waitGroups[0] || [];
1465: 
1466:   if (waitOne.length === GROUP_SIZE) {
1467:     courts[courtNo] = waitOne.slice();
1468:     startedAt[courtNo] = new Date().toISOString();
1469: 
1470:     members.forEach(function(member) {
1471:       if (waitOne.indexOf(member.id) >= 0) {
1472:         member.status = 'playing';
1473:       }
1474:     });
1475: 
1476:     const shifted = [
1477:       (waitGroups[1] || []).slice(),
1478:       (waitGroups[2] || []).slice(),
1479:       (waitGroups[3] || []).slice(),
1480:       (waitGroups[4] || []).slice(),
1481:       []
1482:     ];
1483: 
1484:     writeWaitGroups_(shifted);
1485:   } else {
1486:     courts[courtNo] = [];
1487:     startedAt[courtNo] = '';
1488:   }
1489: 
1490:   writeCourts_(courts, startedAt);
1491:   writeMembers_(members);
1492:   touch_();
1493: 
1494:   return getPublicState();
1495: }

1734: function updateMemberProfile(pin, memberId, name, gender, grade, experience, isNew) {
1735:   auth_(pin);
1736:   return withDocumentLock_('멤버 수정', function() {
1737:     memberId = String(memberId == null ? '' : memberId).trim();
1738:     name = String(name == null ? '' : name).trim();
1739:     gender = String(gender == null ? '' : gender).trim();
1740:     grade = String(grade == null ? '' : grade).trim();
1741:     experience = String(experience == null ? '' : experience).trim();
1742: 
1743:     if (!memberId) throw new Error('수정할 멤버가 없습니다.');
1744:     if (!name) throw new Error('이름 또는 닉네임을 입력하세요.');
1745:     if (name.length > 20) throw new Error('이름은 20자 이내로 입력하세요.');
1746:     if (gender !== 'male' && gender !== 'female') throw new Error('성별을 선택하세요.');
1747:     if (grade.length > 12) throw new Error('급수는 12자 이내로 입력하세요.');
1748:     if (experience.length > 20) throw new Error('구력은 20자 이내로 입력하세요.');
1749: 
1750:     const members = readMembers_();
1751:     const index = members.findIndex(function(item) {
1752:       return String(item.id) === memberId;
1753:     });
1754:     if (index < 0) throw new Error('수정할 기존 멤버를 찾을 수 없습니다.');
1755: 
1756:     const duplicate = members.some(function(item, itemIndex) {
1757:       return itemIndex !== index && String(item.name || '').trim() === name;
1758:     });
1759:     if (duplicate) throw new Error('같은 이름의 멤버가 이미 있습니다.');
1760: 
1761:     // Existing-member-only update: preserve id, game count, status and placement.
1762:     members[index].name = name;
1763:     members[index].gender = gender;
1764:     members[index].grade = grade;
1765:     members[index].experience = experience;
1766:     members[index].isNew = isNew === true || String(isNew || '').toLowerCase() === 'true';
1767: 
1768:     writeMembers_(members);
1769:     if (typeof touch_ === 'function') touch_();
1770:     return getPublicState();
1771:   });
1772: }

1970: function swapCourts(pin, courtA, courtB) {
1971:   return withDocumentLock_(
1972:     '코트 교환',
1973:     function() {
1974:       auth_(pin);
1975: 
1976:       courtA = String(courtA);
1977:       courtB = String(courtB);
1978: 
1979:       if (
1980:         ['1', '2', '3', '4'].indexOf(courtA) < 0 ||
1981:         ['1', '2', '3', '4'].indexOf(courtB) < 0
1982:       ) {
1983:         throw new Error('잘못된 코트 번호입니다.');

1995:       courts[courtB] = temp;
1996:

1998:       startedAt[courtA] = startedAt[courtB];
1999:       startedAt[courtB] = timeTemp;
2000:

2002:       repairData_();
2003:       touch_();
2004: 
2005:       return getPublicState();
2006:     }
2007:   );
2008: }

2085:           throw new Error('첫 번째 대기조에 없는 인원이 선택되었습니다.');
2086:         }
2087:       });
2088: 
2089:       selectedB.forEach(function(id) {
2090:         if (idsB.indexOf(id) < 0) {
2091:           throw new Error('두 번째 대기조에 없는 인원이 선택되었습니다.');
2092:         }
2093:       });
2094: 
2095:       const nextA = idsA.filter(function(id) {
2096:         return selectedA.indexOf(id) < 0;
2097:       }).concat(selectedB);
2098: 
2099:       const nextB = idsB.filter(function(id) {
2100:         return selectedB.indexOf(id) < 0;
2101:       }).concat(selectedA);
2102: 
2103:       if (nextA.length > GROUP_SIZE || nextB.length > GROUP_SIZE) {
2104:         throw new Error(
2105:           '이동 후 한 대기조가 4명을 초과합니다. 선택 인원을 조정하세요.'
2106:         );
2107:       }
2108: 
2109:       waitGroups[groupA] = nextA;
2110:       waitGroups[groupB] = nextB;
2111:       writeWaitGroups_(waitGroups);
2112:       touch_();
2113: 
2114:       return getPublicState();
2115:     }
2116:   );
2117: }

2270: function undoLastAction(pin, state) {
2271:   return withDocumentLock_(
2272:     '직전 작업 실행 취소',
2273:     function() {
2274:       auth_(pin);
2275: 
2276:       if (!state || !Array.isArray(state.members)) {
2277:         throw new Error('되돌릴 상태 정보가 없습니다.');
2278:       }
2279: 
2280:       if (state.members.length > MAX_MEMBERS) {
2281:         throw new Error('멤버 데이터가 허용 범위를 초과했습니다.');
2282:       }
2283: 
2284:       const members = state.members.map(function(member) {
2285:         return {
2286:           id: String(member.id || ''),
2287:           name: String(member.name || '').slice(0, 20),
2288:           gender: member.gender === 'female' ? 'female' : 'male',
2289:           games: Math.max(0, Number(member.games) || 0),
2290:           status: normalizeStatus_(String(member.status || 'active')),
---
2207:       ) {
2208:         throw new Error('잘못된 대기조 번호입니다.');
2209:       }
2210: 
2211:       if (targetMemberId) {
2212:         const other = locate(targetMemberId);
2213:         const sourceGroup = groupOf(source);
2214:         const otherGroup = groupOf(other);
2215: 
2216:         if (sourceGroup) sourceGroup[source.position] = targetMemberId;
2217:         if (otherGroup) otherGroup[other.position] = memberId;
2218: 
2219:         if (!sourceGroup && otherGroup) {
2220:           otherGroup[other.position] = memberId;
2221:         }
2222: 
2223:         if (sourceGroup && !otherGroup) {
2224:           sourceGroup[source.position] = targetMemberId;
2225:         }
2226:       } else {
2227:         const destination = groupOf(targetLocation);
2228: 
2229:         if (destination && destination.length >= GROUP_SIZE) {
2230:           throw new Error('선택한 위치는 이미 4명이 모두 찼습니다.');
2231:         }
2232: 
2233:         const sourceGroup = groupOf(source);
2234:         if (sourceGroup) sourceGroup.splice(source.position, 1);
2235:         if (destination) destination.push(memberId);
2236:       }
2237: 
2238:       Object.keys(courts).forEach(function(key) {
2239:         markCourtStartedIfFull_(courts, startedAt, key);
2240:       });
2241: 
2242:       const playing = {};
2243:       const waiting = {};
2244:       Object.keys(courts).forEach(function(key) {
2245:         (courts[key] || []).forEach(function(id) { playing[id] = true; });
2246:       });
2247:       waitGroups.forEach(function(group) {
2248:         (group || []).forEach(function(id) { waiting[id] = true; });
2249:       });
2250: 
2251:       members.forEach(function(member) {
2252:         if (playing[member.id]) member.status = 'playing';
2253:         else if (waiting[member.id]) member.status = 'waiting';
2254:         else if (
2255:           member.id === memberId ||
2256:           member.id === targetMemberId
2257:         ) member.status = 'active';
2258:       });
2259: 
2260:       writeCourts_(courts, startedAt);
2261:       writeWaitGroups_(waitGroups);
2262:       writeMembers_(members);
2263:       touch_();
2264: 
2265:       return makeState_(members, courts, waitGroups, startedAt);
2266:     }
2267:   );
2268: }

2507:       return String(row[0]).trim() !== '';
2508:     })

2510:       return {
2511:         id: String(row[0]),
2512:         name: String(row[1]),
2513:         gender:
2514:           String(row[2]) === 'female'
2515:             ? 'female'
2516:             : 'male',
2517:         games: Number(row[3]) || 0,
2518:         status: normalizeStatus_(
2519:           String(row[4])
---

2488: function readMembers_() {
2489:   const sheet =
2490:     SpreadsheetApp
2491:       .getActiveSpreadsheet()
2492:       .getSheetByName(SHEET_MEMBERS);
2493: 
2494:   const lastRow = sheet.getLastRow();
2495: 
2496:   if (lastRow < 2) {
2497:     return [];
2498:   }
2499: 
2500:   const values =
2501:     sheet
2502:       .getRange(2, 1, lastRow - 1, 9)
2503:       .getValues();
2504: 
2505:   return values
2506:     .filter(function(row) {
2507:       return String(row[0]).trim() !== '';
2508:     })
2509:     .map(function(row) {
2510:       return {
2511:         id: String(row[0]),
2512:         name: String(row[1]),
2513:         gender:
2514:           String(row[2]) === 'female'
2515:             ? 'female'
2516:             : 'male',
2517:         games: Number(row[3]) || 0,
2518:         status: normalizeStatus_(
2519:           String(row[4])
2520:         ),

378: function unlockVoiceSound() {
379:   try {
380:     const AudioContextClass =
381:       window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext;
382:     if (AudioContextClass) {
383:       VOICE_AUDIO_CONTEXT =
384:         VOICE_AUDIO_CONTEXT || new AudioContextClass();
385:       if (VOICE_AUDIO_CONTEXT.state === 'suspended') {
386:         VOICE_AUDIO_CONTEXT.resume();
387:       }
388:     }
389:   } catch (error) {}
390: 
391:   if (!('speechSynthesis' in window)) return;
392:   try {
393:     window.speechSynthesis.resume();
394:     if (!VOICE_WARMED) {
395:       const utterance =
396:         new SpeechSynthesisUtterance('\u200b');
397:       utterance.lang = 'ko-KR';
398:       utterance.volume = 0;
399:       utterance.onstart = utterance.onend = function() {
400:         VOICE_WARMED = true;
401:         updateSoundUnlockButton();
402:       };
403:       window.speechSynthesis.speak(utterance);
404:     }
405:   } catch (error) {}
406: }
407: 
408: document.addEventListener(
409:   'pointerdown',
410:   unlockVoiceSound,
411:   { passive: true }
412: );

414: function rememberVoiceAnnouncement(courtNo, members) {
415:   LAST_VOICE_ANNOUNCEMENT = {
416:     courtNo: Number(courtNo),
417:     members: (members || []).map(function(member) {
418:       return {
419:         name: String(member.name || ''),
420:         gender:
421:           member.gender === 'female' ? 'female' : 'male'
422:       };
423:     })
424:   };
425:   try {
426:     localStorage.setItem(
427:       LAST_VOICE_KEY,
428:       JSON.stringify(LAST_VOICE_ANNOUNCEMENT)
429:     );
430:   } catch (error) {}
431:   updateReplayVoiceButton();
432: }
433: 
434: function replayLastVoiceAnnouncement() {
435:   if (!LAST_VOICE_ANNOUNCEMENT) {
436:     alert('다시 재생할 경기 종료 안내가 없습니다.');
437:     return;
438:   }
439:   if (!('speechSynthesis' in window)) {
440:     alert(
441:       '현재 브라우저는 음성 안내를 지원하지 않습니다.\n' +
442:       '링크를 Chrome 또는 삼성 인터넷으로 열어 주세요.'
443:     );
444:     return;
445:   }
446:   if (!VOICE_GUIDE_ENABLED) {
447:     VOICE_GUIDE_ENABLED = true;
448:     localStorage.setItem(VOICE_GUIDE_KEY, 'true');
449:     updateVoiceGuideButton();
450:   }
451:   playCourtFinishVoice(
452:     LAST_VOICE_ANNOUNCEMENT.courtNo,
453:     LAST_VOICE_ANNOUNCEMENT.members
454:   );
455: }

457: function updateVoiceRepeatButton() {
458:   const button =
459:     document.getElementById('repeatVoiceButton');

462:   button.setAttribute(
463:     'aria-pressed',
464:     VOICE_REPEAT_ENABLED ? 'true' : 'false'
465:   );
466:   button.textContent =
467:     VOICE_REPEAT_ENABLED ? '🔁 반복 중' : '🔁 반복';
468: }

470: function toggleVoiceRepeat() {
471:   VOICE_REPEAT_ENABLED = !VOICE_REPEAT_ENABLED;
472:   updateVoiceRepeatButton();
473:   if (
474:     VOICE_REPEAT_ENABLED &&
475:     LAST_VOICE_ANNOUNCEMENT &&
476:     !window.speechSynthesis.speaking
477:   ) {
478:     replayLastVoiceAnnouncement();
479:   }
480: }

483:   VOICE_REPEAT_ENABLED = false;

486:   if ('speechSynthesis' in window) {
487:     window.speechSynthesis.cancel();
488:   }
489:   clearVoiceQueueRuntime();
490:   restorePageMediaVolume();
491: }

1072: function handleMemberSelfCardTap(memberId, event) {
1073:   if (IS_ADMIN) return;
1074:   const now = Date.now();
1075:   const same = MEMBER_SELF_LAST_TAP_ID === String(memberId) && now - MEMBER_SELF_LAST_TAP_AT <= 520;
1076:   MEMBER_SELF_LAST_TAP_ID = String(memberId);
1077:   MEMBER_SELF_LAST_TAP_AT = now;
1078:   if (!same) return;
1079:   MEMBER_SELF_LAST_TAP_ID = '';
1080:   MEMBER_SELF_LAST_TAP_AT = 0;
1081:   if (event) { event.preventDefault(); event.stopPropagation(); }
1082:   selectMemberSelf(memberId);
1083:   showMemberSettingMessage('내 이름·알림 ON·진동 ON으로 저장했습니다.', false);
1084: }

1091: function memberLongPressAttributes(memberId) {
1092:   if (!IS_ADMIN) return '';
1093:   return (
1094:     ' onpointerdown="startMemberLongPress(\'' + memberId + '\',event)"' +
1095:     ' onpointermove="moveMemberLongPress(event)"' +
1096:     ' onpointerup="finishMemberLongPress()"' +
1097:     ' onpointercancel="finishMemberLongPress()"' +
1098:     ' oncontextmenu="event.preventDefault()"'
1099:   );
1100: }

1102: function startMemberLongPress(memberId, event) {
1103:   if (!IS_ADMIN) return;
1104:   finishMemberLongPress();
1105: 
1106:   MEMBER_LONG_PRESS_TRACK = {
1107:     memberId: memberId,
1108:     x: event ? event.clientX : 0,
1109:     y: event ? event.clientY : 0
1110:   };
1111: 
1112:   MEMBER_LONG_PRESS_TIMER = setTimeout(function() {
1113:     MEMBER_LONG_PRESS_TIMER = null;
1114:     MEMBER_ACTION_IDS = [memberId];
1115:     MEMBER_LONG_PRESS_BLOCK_UNTIL = Date.now() + 700;
1116: 
1117:     if (navigator.vibrate) {
1118:       navigator.vibrate(25);
1119:     }
1120: 
1121:     document
1122:       .querySelectorAll('[data-member-id="' + memberId + '"]')
1123:       .forEach(function(element) {
1124:         element.classList.add('long-pressed');
1125:       });
1126:     renderQuickMoveBar();
1127:   }, 600);
1128: }
1129: 
1130: function moveMemberLongPress(event) {
1131:   if (!MEMBER_LONG_PRESS_TRACK || !event) return;
1132: 
1133:   if (
1134:     Math.abs(event.clientX - MEMBER_LONG_PRESS_TRACK.x) > 12 ||
1135:     Math.abs(event.clientY - MEMBER_LONG_PRESS_TRACK.y) > 12
1136:   ) {
1137:     finishMemberLongPress();
1138:   }
1139: }
1140:

1143:     clearTimeout(MEMBER_LONG_PRESS_TIMER);

1102: function startMemberLongPress(memberId, event) {
1103:   if (!IS_ADMIN) return;

1105:

1113:     MEMBER_LONG_PRESS_TIMER = null;
1114:     MEMBER_ACTION_IDS = [memberId];

1116:

1118:       navigator.vibrate(25);
1119:     }

1122:       .querySelectorAll('[data-member-id="' + memberId + '"]')

1124:         element.classList.add('long-pressed');

1126:     renderQuickMoveBar();

1130: function moveMemberLongPress(event) {
1131:   if (!MEMBER_LONG_PRESS_TRACK || !event) return;
1132: 
1133:   if (
1134:     Math.abs(event.clientX - MEMBER_LONG_PRESS_TRACK.x) > 12 ||

1137:     finishMemberLongPress();

1144:     MEMBER_LONG_PRESS_TIMER = null;
1145:   }
1146:   MEMBER_LONG_PRESS_TRACK = null;
1147: }

1271: function quickPickMember(type, index, memberId, event) {
1272:   if (event && event.target && event.target.closest('button.small')) return;
1273: 
1274:   if (
1275:     QUICK_PICK &&
1276:     QUICK_PICK.type === type &&
1277:     QUICK_PICK.index === String(index) &&
1278:     QUICK_PICK.memberId === memberId
1279:   ) {
1280:     clearQuickPickOnly();
1281:     return;
1282:   }
1283: 
1284:   if (!QUICK_PICK) {
1285:     QUICK_PICK = { type: type, index: String(index), memberId: memberId };
1286:     document
1287:       .querySelectorAll('[data-member-id="' + memberId + '"]')
1288:       .forEach(function(element) {
1289:         element.classList.add('quick-picked');
1290:       });
1291:     renderQuickMoveBar();
1292:     return;
1293:   }
1294: 
1295:   const source = QUICK_PICK;
1296:   clearQuickPickOnly();
1297: 
1298:   if (source.memberId === memberId) return;
1299:   quickMoveOrSwap(source.memberId, type, index, memberId);
1300: }

1302: function handleExcludedMemberTap(memberId, event) {
1303:   if (!IS_ADMIN) return;
1304:   if (consumeLongPressClick(memberId, event)) return;
1305:   if (assignMemberToChosenEmpty(memberId, event)) return;
1306:   const now = Date.now();
1307:   const isDoubleTap = LAST_EXCLUDED_MEMBER_TAP.memberId === memberId &&
1308:     now - LAST_EXCLUDED_MEMBER_TAP.tappedAt <= 420;
1309:   LAST_EXCLUDED_MEMBER_TAP = isDoubleTap
1310:     ? { memberId:'', tappedAt:0 }
1311:     : { memberId:memberId, tappedAt:now };
1312:   if (isDoubleTap) {
1313:     if (event) { event.preventDefault(); event.stopPropagation(); }
1314:     cancelQuickPick();
1315:     runAction('setMemberStatus', [ADMIN_PIN_VALUE, [memberId], 'active']);
1316:     return;
1317:   }
1318:   toggleSelected(memberId);
1319: }
1320:

1326:   const isDoubleTap = LAST_COURT_MEMBER_TAP.memberId === memberId &&
1327:     now - LAST_COURT_MEMBER_TAP.tappedAt <= 420;

1329:     ? { memberId:'', tappedAt:0 }
1330:     : { memberId:memberId, tappedAt:now };

1332:     if (event) { event.preventDefault(); event.stopPropagation(); }
1333:     cancelQuickPick();
1334:     runAction('setMemberStatus', [ADMIN_PIN_VALUE, [memberId], 'active']);
1335:     return;
1336:   }
1337:   quickPickMember('court', courtNo, memberId, event);
1338: }

1302: function handleExcludedMemberTap(memberId, event) {

1304:   if (consumeLongPressClick(memberId, event)) return;
1305:   if (assignMemberToChosenEmpty(memberId, event)) return;

1307:   const isDoubleTap = LAST_EXCLUDED_MEMBER_TAP.memberId === memberId &&
1308:     now - LAST_EXCLUDED_MEMBER_TAP.tappedAt <= 420;
1309:   LAST_EXCLUDED_MEMBER_TAP = isDoubleTap
1310:     ? { memberId:'', tappedAt:0 }
1311:     : { memberId:memberId, tappedAt:now };

1313:     if (event) { event.preventDefault(); event.stopPropagation(); }
1314:     cancelQuickPick();
1315:     runAction('setMemberStatus', [ADMIN_PIN_VALUE, [memberId], 'active']);
1316:     return;
1317:   }
1318:   toggleSelected(memberId);

1321: function handleCourtMemberTap(courtNo, memberId, event) {
1322:   if (event && event.target && event.target.closest('button.small')) return;
1323:   if (consumeLongPressClick(memberId, event)) return;
1324:   if (assignMemberToChosenEmpty(memberId, event)) return;
1325:   const now = Date.now();
1326:   const isDoubleTap = LAST_COURT_MEMBER_TAP.memberId === memberId &&
1327:     now - LAST_COURT_MEMBER_TAP.tappedAt <= 420;
1328:   LAST_COURT_MEMBER_TAP = isDoubleTap
1329:     ? { memberId:'', tappedAt:0 }
1330:     : { memberId:memberId, tappedAt:now };
1331:   if (isDoubleTap) {
1332:     if (event) { event.preventDefault(); event.stopPropagation(); }
1333:     cancelQuickPick();
1334:     runAction('setMemberStatus', [ADMIN_PIN_VALUE, [memberId], 'active']);
1335:     return;
1336:   }
1337:   quickPickMember('court', courtNo, memberId, event);
1338: }

1440: function locateMemberInState(memberId) {
1441:   for (let courtNo = 1; courtNo <= 4; courtNo++) {
1442:     const position = (STATE.courts[courtNo] || []).indexOf(memberId);
1443:     if (position >= 0) {
1444:       return { type:'court', index:String(courtNo), position:position };
1445:     }
1446:   }
1447:   for (let groupIndex = 0; groupIndex < STATE.waitGroups.length; groupIndex++) {
1448:     const position = (STATE.waitGroups[groupIndex] || []).indexOf(memberId);
1449:     if (position >= 0) {
1450:       return { type:'wait', index:String(groupIndex), position:position };
1451:     }
1452:   }
1453:   return { type:'active', index:'', position:-1 };
1454: }
1455: 
1456: function stateGroup(location) {
1457:   if (location.type === 'court') return STATE.courts[Number(location.index)];
1458:   if (location.type === 'wait') return STATE.waitGroups[Number(location.index)];
1459:   return null;
1460: }

1462: function applyMoveOrSwapLocally(memberId, targetType, targetIndex, targetMemberId) {
1463:   const source = locateMemberInState(memberId);
1464:   const sourceGroup = stateGroup(source);
1465: 
1466:   if (targetMemberId) {
1467:     const other = locateMemberInState(targetMemberId);
1468:     const otherGroup = stateGroup(other);
1469:     if (sourceGroup) sourceGroup[source.position] = targetMemberId;
1470:     if (otherGroup) otherGroup[other.position] = memberId;
1471:   } else {
1472:     const target = { type:targetType, index:String(targetIndex), position:-1 };
1473:     const targetGroup = stateGroup(target);
1474:     if (targetGroup && targetGroup.length >= 4) {
1475:       throw new Error('선택한 위치는 이미 4명이 모두 찼습니다.');
1476:     }
1477:     if (sourceGroup) sourceGroup.splice(source.position, 1);
1478:     if (targetGroup) targetGroup.push(memberId);
1479:   }
1480: 
1481:   const playing = new Set();
1482:   const waiting = new Set();
1483:   Object.keys(STATE.courts).forEach(function(key) {
1484:     (STATE.courts[key] || []).forEach(function(id) { playing.add(id); });
1485:   });
1486:   STATE.waitGroups.forEach(function(group) {
1487:     (group || []).forEach(function(id) { waiting.add(id); });
1488:   });
1489:   STATE.members.forEach(function(member) {
1490:     if (playing.has(member.id)) member.status = 'playing';
1491:     else if (waiting.has(member.id)) member.status = 'waiting';
1492:     else if (member.id === memberId || member.id === targetMemberId) {
1493:       member.status = 'active';
1494:     }
1495:   });
1496: }

1505:       });

1513:     return (group || []).filter(function(id) {
1514:       return !moving.has(id);
1515:     });
1516:   });
1517: 
1518:   if (targetType === 'court') {
1519:     const courtNo = Number(targetIndex);

1437:   );

1522:     STATE.courtStartedAt[courtNo] =
1523:       STATE.courts[courtNo].length === 4
1524:         ? new Date().toISOString()
1525:         : '';
1526:   } else {
1527:     const groupIndex = Number(targetIndex);
---
1457:   if (location.type === 'court') return STATE.courts[Number(location.index)];
1458:   if (location.type === 'wait') return STATE.waitGroups[Number(location.index)];
1459:   return null;
1460: }

1462: function applyMoveOrSwapLocally(memberId, targetType, targetIndex, targetMemberId) {
1463:   const source = locateMemberInState(memberId);
1464:   const sourceGroup = stateGroup(source);
1465: 
1466:   if (targetMemberId) {
1467:     const other = locateMemberInState(targetMemberId);
1468:     const otherGroup = stateGroup(other);
1469:     if (sourceGroup) sourceGroup[source.position] = targetMemberId;
1470:     if (otherGroup) otherGroup[other.position] = memberId;
1471:   } else {
1472:     const target = { type:targetType, index:String(targetIndex), position:-1 };
1473:     const targetGroup = stateGroup(target);
1474:     if (targetGroup && targetGroup.length >= 4) {
1475:       throw new Error('선택한 위치는 이미 4명이 모두 찼습니다.');
1476:     }
1477:     if (sourceGroup) sourceGroup.splice(source.position, 1);
1478:     if (targetGroup) targetGroup.push(memberId);
1479:   }
1480: 
1481:   const playing = new Set();
1482:   const waiting = new Set();
1483:   Object.keys(STATE.courts).forEach(function(key) {
1484:     (STATE.courts[key] || []).forEach(function(id) { playing.add(id); });
1485:   });
1486:   STATE.waitGroups.forEach(function(group) {
1487:     (group || []).forEach(function(id) { waiting.add(id); });
1488:   });
1489:   STATE.members.forEach(function(member) {
1490:     if (playing.has(member.id)) member.status = 'playing';
1491:     else if (waiting.has(member.id)) member.status = 'waiting';
1492:     else if (member.id === memberId || member.id === targetMemberId) {
1493:       member.status = 'active';
1494:     }
1495:   });
1496: }

1523:       STATE.courts[courtNo].length === 4
1524:         ? new Date().toISOString()
1525:         : '';

1527:     const groupIndex = Number(targetIndex);

1529:       (STATE.waitGroups[groupIndex] || []).concat(ids);

1538:     });
1539:   });

1513:     return (group || []).filter(function(id) {
1514:       return !moving.has(id);
1515:     });
1516:   });
1517: 
1518:   if (targetType === 'court') {
1519:     const courtNo = Number(targetIndex);
1520:     STATE.courts[courtNo] =
1521:       (STATE.courts[courtNo] || []).concat(ids);
1522:     STATE.courtStartedAt[courtNo] =
1523:       STATE.courts[courtNo].length === 4
1524:         ? new Date().toISOString()
1525:         : '';
1526:   } else {
1527:     const groupIndex = Number(targetIndex);
1528:     STATE.waitGroups[groupIndex] =
1529:       (STATE.waitGroups[groupIndex] || []).concat(ids);
1530:   }
1531: 
1532:   const playing = new Set();
1533:   const waiting = new Set();
1534: 
1535:   Object.keys(STATE.courts).forEach(function(key) {
1536:     (STATE.courts[key] || []).forEach(function(id) {
1537:       playing.add(id);
1538:     });
1539:   });
1540:   STATE.waitGroups.forEach(function(group) {
1541:     (group || []).forEach(function(id) {
1542:       waiting.add(id);
1543:     });
1544:   });
1545: 
1546:   STATE.members.forEach(function(member) {
1547:     if (playing.has(member.id)) member.status = 'playing';
1548:     else if (waiting.has(member.id)) member.status = 'waiting';
1549:     else if (moving.has(member.id)) member.status = 'active';
1550:   });
1551: }

1498: function applyBatchAssignLocally(ids, targetType, targetIndex) {
1499:   const moving = new Set(ids);
1500: 
1501:   Object.keys(STATE.courts).forEach(function(key) {
1502:     STATE.courts[key] =
1503:       (STATE.courts[key] || []).filter(function(id) {
1504:         return !moving.has(id);
1505:       });
1506: 
1507:     if (STATE.courts[key].length < 4) {
1508:       STATE.courtStartedAt[key] = '';
1509:     }
1510:   });
1511: 
1512:   STATE.waitGroups = STATE.waitGroups.map(function(group) {
1513:     return (group || []).filter(function(id) {
1514:       return !moving.has(id);
1515:     });
1516:   });
1517: 
1518:   if (targetType === 'court') {
1519:     const courtNo = Number(targetIndex);
1520:     STATE.courts[courtNo] =
1521:       (STATE.courts[courtNo] || []).concat(ids);
1522:     STATE.courtStartedAt[courtNo] =
1523:       STATE.courts[courtNo].length === 4
1524:         ? new Date().toISOString()
1525:         : '';
1526:   } else {
1527:     const groupIndex = Number(targetIndex);
1528:     STATE.waitGroups[groupIndex] =
1529:       (STATE.waitGroups[groupIndex] || []).concat(ids);
1530:   }
1531: 
1532:   const playing = new Set();
1533:   const waiting = new Set();
1534: 
1535:   Object.keys(STATE.courts).forEach(function(key) {
1536:     (STATE.courts[key] || []).forEach(function(id) {
1537:       playing.add(id);
1538:     });
1539:   });
1540:   STATE.waitGroups.forEach(function(group) {
1541:     (group || []).forEach(function(id) {
1542:       waiting.add(id);
1543:     });
1544:   });
1545: 
1546:   STATE.members.forEach(function(member) {
1547:     if (playing.has(member.id)) member.status = 'playing';
1548:     else if (waiting.has(member.id)) member.status = 'waiting';
1549:     else if (moving.has(member.id)) member.status = 'active';
1550:   });
1551: }

1585: async function quickMoveOrSwap(memberId, targetType, targetIndex, targetMemberId) {
1586:   const previousState = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(STATE));
1587:   try {
1588:     applyMoveOrSwapLocally(
1589:       memberId,
1590:       targetType,
1591:       targetIndex,
1592:       targetMemberId || ''
1593:     );
1594:     renderState();
1595:     const state = await server('moveOrSwapMember', [
1596:       ADMIN_PIN_VALUE,
1597:       memberId,
1598:       targetType,
1599:       String(targetIndex),
1600:       targetMemberId || ''
1601:     ]);
1602:     renderState(state);
1603:     setUndoState(previousState);
1604:   } catch (error) {
1605:     STATE = previousState;
1606:     renderState();
1607:     alert(error.message || error);
1608:   }
1609: }

1612:   const bar = document.getElementById('quickMoveBar');
1613:   if (!bar) return;
1614:   bar.classList.toggle(
1615:     'hidden',

1617:   );

1619:   const member =
1620:     MEMBER_ACTION_IDS.length === 1
1621:       ? memberById(MEMBER_ACTION_IDS[0])
1622:       : null;
1623:   if (name) {
1624:     name.textContent =
1625:       member
1626:         ? member.name
1627:         : MEMBER_ACTION_IDS.length
1628:           ? MEMBER_ACTION_IDS.length + '명 선택'
1629:           : '선택 인원';
1630:   }

1693: function assignToQuickTarget(type, index) {

1695:     const memberId = QUICK_PICK.memberId;
1696:     cancelQuickPick();

1973: function unlockMemberAlertAudio() {

1977:       window.__jayumintonMemberAudioContext = new AudioContextClass();

1987: function memberSelfSearchMatches() {

1990:   if (!query) return [];
1991:   return sortMembersByKoreanName((STATE.members || []).filter(function(member) {
1992:     return member && String(member.name || '').toLocaleLowerCase('ko-KR').indexOf(query) >= 0;
1993:   })).slice(0, 8);
1994: }

1997:   if (IS_ADMIN) return;

2014: }

2209: function detectMemberInstallPlatform() {
2210:   if (MEMBER_APP_INSTALL_STATE.platform === 'android' || MEMBER_APP_INSTALL_STATE.platform === 'ios') {
2211:     return MEMBER_APP_INSTALL_STATE.platform;
2212:   }
2213:   const ua = String(navigator.userAgent || '');
2214:   if (/Android/i.test(ua)) return 'android';
2215:   if (/iPhone|iPad|iPod/i.test(ua) ||
2216:       (navigator.platform === 'MacIntel' && Number(navigator.maxTouchPoints || 0) > 1)) return 'ios';
2217:   return 'other';
2218: }
2219: 
2220: function detectMemberInstallBrowserContext() {
2221:   const known = String(MEMBER_APP_INSTALL_STATE.browserContext || '');
2222:   if (known && known !== 'other') return known;
2223:   const ua = String(navigator.userAgent || '');
2224:   const ref = String(document.referrer || '');
2225:   const embedded = /;\s*wv\)|\bwv\b|KAKAOTALK|KakaoTalk|DaumApps|NAVER|Instagram|FBAN|FBAV|Line\/|Daangn|Danggeun|Karrot|com\.towneers|EverytimeApp|WhaleInApp/i.test(ua) ||
2226:     /kakao|daangn|danggeun|karrot|naver|instagram/i.test(ref) || !!window.NativeVoice;
2227:   const platform = detectMemberInstallPlatform();
2228:   if (embedded && platform === 'android') return 'android-embedded';
2229:   if (embedded && platform === 'ios') return 'ios-embedded';
2230:   if (platform === 'android') return 'android-browser';
2231:   if (platform === 'ios') return 'ios-safari';
2232:   return 'other';
2233: }

2235: function navigateMemberTop(url) {
2236:   try {
2237:     window.top.location.href = url;
2238:     return true;
2239:   } catch (error) {
2240:     try {
2241:       window.location.href = url;
2242:       return true;
2243:     } catch (innerError) {
2244:       return false;
2245:     }
2246:   }
2247: }

2249: function openMemberInstallInChrome(installUrl) {
2250:   if (isUnifiedMemberFrame()) {
2251:     postUnifiedMemberMessage('JAYUMINTON_APP_INSTALL_REQUEST', {
2252:       platform: 'android',
2253:       browserContext: detectMemberInstallBrowserContext(),
2254:       installUrl: installUrl
2255:     });
2256:     return;
2257:   }
2258:   try {
2259:     const target = new URL(installUrl);
2260:     if (target.origin !== UNIFIED_MEMBER_APP_ORIGIN) throw new Error('invalid member app origin');
2261:     target.searchParams.set('install', 'choose');
2262:     target.searchParams.set('external', '1');
2263:     target.searchParams.set('source', 'embedded');
2264:     target.searchParams.set('app', 'user');
2265:     target.searchParams.set('mode', 'user');
2266:     navigateMemberTop(target.toString());
2267:     return;
2268:   } catch (error) {}
2269:   navigateMemberTop(UNIFIED_MEMBER_APP_URL + '?install=choose&external=1&source=embedded&app=user&mode=user');
2270: }

2277:   } catch (error) {}
2278:   navigateMemberTop(installUrl);

2381: function currentWebPushConnection() {
2382:   try {
2383:     const saved = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(WEB_PUSH_CONNECTION_KEY) || 'null');
2384:     return saved && saved.connected && saved.memberId
2385:       ? saved
2386:       : null;
2387:   } catch (error) {
2388:     return null;
2389:   }
2390: }

2392: function applyWebPushReturn() {
2393:   if (IS_ADMIN) return;
2394:   let returned = window.JAYUMINTON_PUSH_RETURN || {};
2395:   try {
2396:     const prefix = 'JAYUMINTON_PUSH_RETURN:';
2397:     if ((!returned || !returned.connected) &&
2398:         String(window.name || '').indexOf(prefix) === 0) {
2399:       const rawReturn = String(window.name).slice(prefix.length);
2400:       try {
2401:         returned = JSON.parse(rawReturn);
2402:       } catch (parseError) {
2403:         returned = JSON.parse(decodeURIComponent(rawReturn));
2404:       }
2405:       window.name = '';
2406:     }
2407:   } catch (error) {
2408:     returned = returned || {};
2409:   }
2410:   if (!returned.connected || !returned.memberId) return;
2411:   const connection = {
2412:     connected: true,
2413:     memberId: String(returned.memberId),
2414:     memberName: String(returned.memberName || '').trim(),
2415:     connectedAt: Date.now()
2416:   };
2417:   try {
2418:     localStorage.setItem(WEB_PUSH_CONNECTION_KEY, JSON.stringify(connection));
2419:     localStorage.setItem(WEB_PUSH_SELECTED_MEMBER_KEY, JSON.stringify({
2420:       id: connection.memberId,
2421:       name: connection.memberName
2422:     }));
2423:   } catch (error) {}
2424: }

2434: function encodeUnifiedHandoff(value) {
2435:   try {
2436:     const bytes = new TextEncoder().encode(JSON.stringify(value));
2437:     let binary = '';
2438:     bytes.forEach(function(byte) { binary += String.fromCharCode(byte); });
2439:     return btoa(binary).replace(/\+/g, '-').replace(/\//g, '_').replace(/=+$/g, '');
2440:   } catch (error) {
2441:     return '';
2442:   }
2443: }
2444: 
2445: function webPushEscapeHtml(value) {
2446:   return String(value == null ? '' : value)
2447:     .replace(/&/g, '&amp;')
2448:     .replace(/</g, '&lt;')
2449:     .replace(/>/g, '&gt;')
2450:     .replace(/"/g, '&quot;')
2451:     .replace(/'/g, '&#39;');
2452: }

2434: function encodeUnifiedHandoff(value) {
2435:   try {
2436:     const bytes = new TextEncoder().encode(JSON.stringify(value));
2437:     let binary = '';
2438:     bytes.forEach(function(byte) { binary += String.fromCharCode(byte); });
2439:     return btoa(binary).replace(/\+/g, '-').replace(/\//g, '_').replace(/=+$/g, '');
2440:   } catch (error) {
2441:     return '';
2442:   }
2443: }

2742:     if (!clickedMember) return;
2743:     if (window.parent !== window) {
2744:       window.parent.postMessage({
2745:         type:'JAYUMINTON_MEMBER_CLICKED',
2746:         member:{id:String(clickedMember.id), name:String(clickedMember.name || '')}
2747:       }, '*');
2748:     }
2749:   }, true);
2750: 
2751:   function reportUnifiedContentHeight() {
2752:     if (window.parent === window) return;
2753:     const height = Math.max(
2754:       document.body ? document.body.scrollHeight : 0,
2755:       document.documentElement ? document.documentElement.scrollHeight : 0
2756:     );
2757:     window.parent.postMessage({type:'JAYUMINTON_CONTENT_HEIGHT', height:height}, '*');
2758:   }
2759:   window.addEventListener('load', reportUnifiedContentHeight);
2760:   window.addEventListener('resize', reportUnifiedContentHeight);
2761:   if ('ResizeObserver' in window) {
2762:     new ResizeObserver(reportUnifiedContentHeight).observe(document.body);
2763:   }
2764:   setInterval(reportUnifiedContentHeight, 2500);
2765: }
2766: 
2767: function memberLocation(state, memberId) {
2768:   if (!state || !memberId) return {type: 'none', index: -1};
2769:   const id = String(memberId);
2770:   for (let courtNo = 1; courtNo <= 4; courtNo += 1) {
2771:     if ((state.courts && state.courts[courtNo] || []).some(function(value) {
2772:       return String(value) === id;
2773:     })) return {type: 'court', index: courtNo};
2774:   }
2775:   const waits = Array.isArray(state.waitGroups) ? state.waitGroups : [];
2776:   for (let waitIndex = 0; waitIndex < waits.length; waitIndex += 1) {
2777:     if ((waits[waitIndex] || []).some(function(value) {
2778:       return String(value) === id;
2779:     })) return {type: 'wait', index: waitIndex};
2780:   }
2781:   return {type: 'other', index: -1};
2782: }
2783: 
2784: function mostElapsedCourtNoForState(state) {
2785:   const candidates = [1, 2, 3, 4].filter(function(no) {
2786:     return state && state.courts && Array.isArray(state.courts[no]) &&
2787:       state.courts[no].length === 4;
2788:   }).map(function(no) {
2789:     const started = parseCourtStart(state.courtStartedAt && state.courtStartedAt[no]);
2790:     return {courtNo: no, startedAt: started ? started.getTime() : Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY};
---
2762:     new ResizeObserver(reportUnifiedContentHeight).observe(document.body);
2763:   }
2764:   setInterval(reportUnifiedContentHeight, 2500);
2765: }

2767: function memberLocation(state, memberId) {
2768:   if (!state || !memberId) return {type: 'none', index: -1};
2769:   const id = String(memberId);
2770:   for (let courtNo = 1; courtNo <= 4; courtNo += 1) {

2772:       return String(value) === id;
2773:     })) return {type: 'court', index: courtNo};
2774:   }
2775:   const waits = Array.isArray(state.waitGroups) ? state.waitGroups : [];
2776:   for (let waitIndex = 0; waitIndex < waits.length; waitIndex += 1) {
2777:     if ((waits[waitIndex] || []).some(function(value) {

2784: function mostElapsedCourtNoForState(state) {
2785:   const candidates = [1, 2, 3, 4].filter(function(no) {
2786:     return state && state.courts && Array.isArray(state.courts[no]) &&
2787:       state.courts[no].length === 4;
2788:   }).map(function(no) {
2789:     const started = parseCourtStart(state.courtStartedAt && state.courtStartedAt[no]);
2790:     return {courtNo: no, startedAt: started ? started.getTime() : Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY};
2791:   }).sort(function(a, b) {
2792:     return a.startedAt - b.startedAt || a.courtNo - b.courtNo;
2793:   });
2794:   return candidates.length ? candidates[0].courtNo : 0;
2795: }

2799:   if (type === 'wait1_ready') return 3;

2807: function playSingleMemberAlertCue(type) {
2808:   try {
2809:     if (memberVibrationEnabled() && navigator.vibrate) {
2810:       navigator.vibrate(memberVibrationPattern(type));
2811:     }
2812:   } catch (error) {}
2813:   try {
2814:     if (!memberAlertEnabled()) return;
2815:     const ctx = window.__jayumintonMemberAudioContext;
2816:     if (!ctx || !MEMBER_ALERT_AUDIO_READY) return;
2817:     const oscillator = ctx.createOscillator();
2818:     const gain = ctx.createGain();
2819:     oscillator.frequency.value = 880;
2820:     gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.0001, ctx.currentTime);
2821:     gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.18, ctx.currentTime + 0.02);
2822:     gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.0001, ctx.currentTime + 0.45);
2823:     oscillator.connect(gain).connect(ctx.destination);
2824:     oscillator.start();
2825:     oscillator.stop(ctx.currentTime + 0.48);
2826:   } catch (error) {}
2827: }

2855:     titleElement.textContent = title;
2856:     bodyElement.textContent = body;
2857:     box.classList.remove('hidden');
2858:   }
2859:   const connectedPush = !IS_ADMIN && window.parent !== window && currentWebPushConnection();
2860:   if (!connectedPush) {
2861:     playMemberAlertCue(type, title, body, eventId);
2862:   }
2863: }

2901: async function refreshMemberState() {
2902:   if (IS_ADMIN) return;
2903:   const button = document.getElementById('memberRefreshButton');
2904:   if (button) {
2905:     button.disabled = true;
2906:     button.textContent = '갱신 중...';
2907:   }
2908:   try {
2909:     await loadState();
2910:   } catch (error) {
2911:     alert(error.message || error);
2912:   } finally {
2913:     if (button) {
2914:       button.disabled = false;
2915:       button.textContent = '↻ 현황 갱신';
2916:     }
2917:   }
2918: }
2919: 
2920: function renderState(state) {
2921:   if (state) {
2922:     STATE = normalizeStateMemberProfiles(state);
2923:   } else {
2924:     normalizeStateMemberProfiles(STATE);
2925:   }
2926: 
2927:   if (!IS_ADMIN && window.parent !== window) {
2928:     window.parent.postMessage({
2929:       type:'JAYUMINTON_MEMBER_LIST',
2930:       members:(STATE.members || []).map(function(item) {
2931:         return {id:String(item.id), name:String(item.name || '')};

2901: async function refreshMemberState() {
2902:   if (IS_ADMIN) return;
2903:   const button = document.getElementById('memberRefreshButton');
2904:   if (button) {
2905:     button.disabled = true;
2906:     button.textContent = '갱신 중...';
2907:   }
2908:   try {
2909:     await loadState();
2910:   } catch (error) {
2911:     alert(error.message || error);
2912:   } finally {
2913:     if (button) {
2914:       button.disabled = false;
2915:       button.textContent = '↻ 현황 갱신';
2916:     }
2917:   }

5190:   gender.value = member.gender === 'female' ? 'female' : 'male';
5191:   grade.value = String(member.grade || '');
5192:   experience.value = String(member.experience || '');
5193:   isNew.checked = member.isNew === true;
5194:   updateButton.disabled = false;
5195:   closeMemberActionBar();

5197:   if (details) details.open = true;

5883: assignMemberToChosenEmpty = function(memberId, event) {
5884:   pruneEmptySlotTargets();
5885:   if (!EMPTY_SLOT_TARGETS.length) return false;
5886: 
5887:   const target = copyEmptySlotTarget(EMPTY_SLOT_TARGETS[0]);
5888:   const targetGroup = targetGroupForEmpty(target);
5889: 
5890:   if (targetGroup.indexOf(memberId) >= 0) {
5891:     alert('이미 선택한 위치에 있는 멤버입니다.');
5892:     return true;
5893:   }
5894: 
5895:   if (targetGroup.length >= 4) {
5896:     removeEmptyTargetsForGroup(target.type, target.index);
5897:     renderCourts();
5898:     renderWaitGroups();
5899:     return assignMemberToChosenEmpty(memberId, event);
5900:   }
5901: 
5902:   if (event) {
5903:     event.preventDefault();
5904:     event.stopPropagation();
5905:   }
5906:

5995:     return;
5996:   }
5997:   if (ids.length !== needed) {
5998:     alert('현재 인원과 남복·여복·혼복을 만들 수 있는 코트배정 대기 인원이 부족합니다.');
5999:     return;
6000:   }
6001: 
6002:   const finalMembers = existingMembers.concat(ids.map(memberById).filter(Boolean));
6003:   if (!isAllowedDoublesTeam(finalMembers)) {

26: /**
27:  * Handles:
28:  * 1) Public browser token registration/unregistration.
29:  * 2) Protected admin assignment events.
30:  */
31: function doPost(e) {
32:   try {
33:     const rawBody = e && e.parameter && e.parameter.payload
34:       ? String(e.parameter.payload || '')
35:       : String(e && e.postData ? (e.postData.contents || '') : '');
36:     const body = JSON.parse(rawBody || '{}');
37:     const action = String(body.action || '').trim();
38: 
39:     if (action === 'register_web_token') {
40:       return jsonOutput_(registerWebToken_(body));
41:     }
42:     if (action === 'unregister_web_token') {
43:       return jsonOutput_(unregisterWebToken_(body));
44:     }
45:     if (action === 'test_native_push') {
46:       return jsonOutput_(testNativePush_(body));
47:     }
48: 
49:     verifyAdminSecret_(e);
50:     const event = cleanEvent_(body);
51:     return jsonOutput_(sendAssignmentEvent_(event));
52:   } catch (error) {
53:     console.error(error && error.stack ? error.stack : String(error));
54:     return jsonOutput_({
55:       ok: false,
56:       error: String(error && error.message ? error.message : error)
57:     });
58:   }
59: }

62: function testNativePush_(body) {
63:   const memberId = cleanText_(body.memberId, 200);
64:   const memberName = cleanText_(body.memberName, 80) || '사용자';

67:     throw new Error('memberId and token are required for native push test.');
68:   }
69: 
70:   const registeredForMember = loadWebPushTokens_().some(function(record) {
71:     return record && String(record.memberId || '') === memberId && record.token === token;
72:   });
73:   if (!registeredForMember) {
74:     return {
75:       ok: false,
76:       stage: 'registration_lookup',
77:       status: 0,
78:       messageId: '',
79:       error: 'TOKEN_NOT_REGISTERED_FOR_MEMBER'
80:     };

82: 
83:   // Possession of the device FCM token is required.  The endpoint sends only
84:   // a fixed diagnostic message back to that same device; callers cannot
85:   // choose arbitrary notification content.
86:   const now = Date.now();
87:   const event = {
88:     type: 'court_assignment',
89:     assignmentId: 'native-test-' + now,
90:     courtNo: '테스트',
91:     expectedCourtNo: '',
92:     members: [{id: memberId, name: memberName}]
93:   };
94:   const member = {id: memberId, name: memberName};
95:   const request = makeWebFcmRequest_(event, member, token, getFcmAccessToken_());
96:   const requestOptions = Object.assign({}, request);
97:   delete requestOptions.url;
98:   const response = UrlFetchApp.fetch(request.url, requestOptions);
99:   const status = response.getResponseCode();
100:   const responseText = response.getContentText();
101:   let messageId = '';
102:   let fcmError = '';
103:   try {
104:     const parsed = JSON.parse(responseText || '{}');
105:     messageId = String(parsed.name || '');
106:     fcmError = String(parsed.error && (parsed.error.status || parsed.error.message) || '');
107:   } catch (_) {}
108:   return {
109:     ok: status >= 200 && status < 300 && Boolean(messageId),
110:     stage: 'fcm_send',

67:     throw new Error('memberId and token are required for native push test.');
68:   }
69: 
70:   const registeredForMember = loadWebPushTokens_().some(function(record) {
71:     return record && String(record.memberId || '') === memberId && record.token === token;
72:   });

74:     return {
75:       ok: false,
76:       stage: 'registration_lookup',
77:       status: 0,
78:       messageId: '',
79:       error: 'TOKEN_NOT_REGISTERED_FOR_MEMBER'

88:     type: 'court_assignment',
89:     assignmentId: 'native-test-' + now,

91:     expectedCourtNo: '',
92:     members: [{id: memberId, name: memberName}]
93:   };
94:   const member = {id: memberId, name: memberName};
95:   const request = makeWebFcmRequest_(event, member, token, getFcmAccessToken_());
96:   const requestOptions = Object.assign({}, request);
97:   delete requestOptions.url;
98:   const response = UrlFetchApp.fetch(request.url, requestOptions);
99:   const status = response.getResponseCode();
100:   const responseText = response.getContentText();
101:   let messageId = '';
102:   let fcmError = '';
103:   try {
104:     const parsed = JSON.parse(responseText || '{}');
105:     messageId = String(parsed.name || '');
106:     fcmError = String(parsed.error && (parsed.error.status || parsed.error.message) || '');
107:   } catch (_) {}
108:   return {
109:     ok: status >= 200 && status < 300 && Boolean(messageId),
110:     stage: 'fcm_send',
111:     status: status,
112:     messageId: messageId,
113:     error: fcmError
114:   };
115: }

117: function verifyPushConfiguration() {
118:   const properties = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties();
119:   const sharedSecret = String(
120:     properties.getProperty('JAYUMINTON_PUSH_SHARED_SECRET') || ''
121:   );
122:   const serviceAccountJson = String(
123:     properties.getProperty('FCM_SERVICE_ACCOUNT_JSON') || ''
124:   );
125: 
126:   if (!sharedSecret) {
127:     throw new Error('JAYUMINTON_PUSH_SHARED_SECRET Script Property is missing.');
128:   }
129:   if (!serviceAccountJson) {
130:     throw new Error('FCM_SERVICE_ACCOUNT_JSON Script Property is missing.');
131:   }
132: 
133:   const credentials = JSON.parse(serviceAccountJson);
134:   if (credentials.project_id !== JAYUMINTON_PUSH_CONFIG.projectId) {
135:     throw new Error('Service-account project_id must be jayuminton-push.');
136:   }
137: 
138:   const token = getFcmAccessToken_(true);
139:   return {
140:     ok: Boolean(token),
141:     projectId: credentials.project_id,
142:     clientEmail: credentials.client_email,
143:     registeredTokens: countWebPushTokens_()
144:   };
145: }

152:

154:     throw new Error('memberId, memberName and token are required.');
155:   }
156: 
157:   const lock = LockService.getScriptLock();
158:   lock.waitLock(10000);
159:   try {
160:     const records = loadWebPushTokens_();
161:     const now = Date.now();
162:     const active = records.filter(function(record) {
163:       return record &&
164:         record.token &&
165:         now - Number(record.updatedAt || 0) <=
166:           JAYUMINTON_PUSH_CONFIG.tokenMaxAgeMs &&
167:         record.token !== token;
168:     });
169: 
170:     active.push({
171:       memberId: memberId,
172:       memberName: memberName,
173:       token: token,
174:       userAgent: userAgent,
175:       updatedAt: now
176:     });
177: 
178:     const limited = limitWebPushTokens_(active);
179:     saveWebPushTokens_(limited);
180: 
181:     return {
182:       ok: true,
183:       action: 'registered',
184:       memberId: memberId,
185:       memberName: memberName,
186:       registeredTokens: limited.length
187:     };
188:   } finally {
189:     lock.releaseLock();
190:   }

193: function unregisterWebToken_(body) {
194:   const token = cleanToken_(body.token);
195:   if (!token) {
196:     throw new Error('token is required.');
197:   }
198: 
199:   const lock = LockService.getScriptLock();
200:   lock.waitLock(10000);
201:   try {
202:     const records = loadWebPushTokens_();
203:     const next = records.filter(function(record) {
204:       return record && record.token !== token;
205:     });
206:     saveWebPushTokens_(next);
207:     return {
208:       ok: true,
209:       action: 'unregistered',
210:       removed: records.length - next.length
211:     };
212:   } finally {
213:     lock.releaseLock();
214:   }
215: }

262:   let sent = 0;
263:   responses.forEach(function(response, index) {
264:     const status = response.getResponseCode();
265:     if (status >= 200 && status < 300) {
266:       sent += 1;
267:       return;
268:     }
269: 
270:     const text = response.getContentText();
271:     const target = targets[index];
272:     console.error(JSON.stringify({
273:       message: 'FCM web send failed',
274:       assignmentId: event.assignmentId,
275:       memberId: target.member.id,
276:       status: status,
277:       response: text
278:     }));
279: 
280:     if (status === 404 || status === 410 ||
281:         /UNREGISTERED|registration-token-not-registered/i.test(text)) {
282:       failedTokens.push(target.token);
283:     }
284:   });
285: 
286:   if (failedTokens.length) {
287:     removeInvalidTokens_(failedTokens);
288:   }
289: 
290:   return {
291:     ok: sent === targets.length,
292:     assignmentId: event.assignmentId,
293:     sent: sent,
294:     failed: targets.length - sent
295:   };
296: }

298: function makeWebFcmRequest_(event, member, token, accessToken) {
299:   const isWait = event.type === 'wait1_ready';
---
217: function sendAssignmentEvent_(event) {
218:   const records = loadWebPushTokens_();
219:   const now = Date.now();
220:   const memberIds = {};
221:   event.members.forEach(function(member) {
222:     memberIds[member.id] = member;
223:   });
224: 
225:   const targets = [];
226:   const seenTokens = {};
227:   records.forEach(function(record) {
228:     if (!record || !record.token || seenTokens[record.token]) return;
229:     if (now - Number(record.updatedAt || 0) >
230:         JAYUMINTON_PUSH_CONFIG.tokenMaxAgeMs) return;
231:     const member = memberIds[String(record.memberId || '')];
232:     if (!member) return;
233:     seenTokens[record.token] = true;
234:     targets.push({
235:       token: record.token,
236:       member: member
237:     });
238:   });
239: 
240:   if (!targets.length) {
241:     return {
242:       ok: true,
243:       assignmentId: event.assignmentId,
244:       sent: 0,
245:       failed: 0,
246:       noRegisteredBrowser: event.members.length
247:     };
248:   }
249: 
250:   const accessToken = getFcmAccessToken_();
251:   const requests = targets.map(function(target) {
252:     return makeWebFcmRequest_(
253:       event,
254:       target.member,
255:       target.token,
256:       accessToken
257:     );
258:   });
259:   const responses = UrlFetchApp.fetchAll(requests);
260: 
261:   const failedTokens = [];
262:   let sent = 0;
263:   responses.forEach(function(response, index) {
264:     const status = response.getResponseCode();
265:     if (status >= 200 && status < 300) {
266:       sent += 1;
267:       return;
268:     }
269: 
270:     const text = response.getContentText();
271:     const target = targets[index];
272:     console.error(JSON.stringify({
273:       message: 'FCM web send failed',
274:       assignmentId: event.assignmentId,
275:       memberId: target.member.id,
276:       status: status,
277:       response: text
278:     }));
279: 
280:     if (status === 404 || status === 410 ||
281:         /UNREGISTERED|registration-token-not-registered/i.test(text)) {
282:       failedTokens.push(target.token);
283:     }
284:   });
285: 
286:   if (failedTokens.length) {
287:     removeInvalidTokens_(failedTokens);
288:   }
289: 
290:   return {
291:     ok: sent === targets.length,
292:     assignmentId: event.assignmentId,
293:     sent: sent,
294:     failed: targets.length - sent
295:   };
296: }

373: function cleanEvent_(body) {
374:   const type = String(body && body.type || 'court_assignment').trim();
375:   const assignmentId = String(body && body.assignmentId || '').trim();
376:   const sourceMembers = Array.isArray(body && body.members)
377:     ? body.members
378:     : [];
379: 
380:   if (!/^[A-Za-z0-9_.-]{8,500}$/.test(assignmentId)) {
381:     throw new Error('Invalid assignmentId.');
382:   }
383:   if (type !== 'court_assignment' && type !== 'wait1_ready') {
384:     throw new Error('Invalid notification type.');
385:   }
386: 
387:   const uniqueMembers = {};
388:   sourceMembers.forEach(function(member) {
389:     const id = cleanText_(member && member.id, 200);
390:     const name = cleanText_(member && member.name, 80);
391:     if (id && name) {
392:       uniqueMembers[id] = {id: id, name: name};
393:     }
394:   });
395:   const members = Object.keys(uniqueMembers).map(function(id) {
396:     return uniqueMembers[id];
397:   });
398: 
399:   if (type === 'court_assignment') {
400:     const courtNo = Number(body.courtNo);
401:     if ([1, 2, 3, 4].indexOf(courtNo) === -1 ||
402:         members.length !== 4) {
403:       throw new Error('Court assignment requires one valid court and four members.');
404:     }
405:     return {
406:       type: type,
407:       assignmentId: assignmentId,
408:       courtNo: courtNo,
409:       expectedCourtNo: 0,
410:       members: members
411:     };
412:   }
413: 
414:   const expectedCourtNo = Number(body.expectedCourtNo);
415:   if ([1, 2, 3, 4].indexOf(expectedCourtNo) === -1 ||
416:       members.length < 1 || members.length > 4) {
417:     throw new Error('Wait-1 notification requires one expected court and one to four members.');
418:   }
---
339:           channel_id: event.type === 'court_assignment'
340:             ? 'jayuminton_court_system_v114'
341:             : 'jayuminton_wait1_system_v114',
342:           notification_priority: 'PRIORITY_MAX',
343:           visibility: 'PUBLIC',
344:           vibrate_timings: event.type === 'court_assignment'
345:             ? ['0s','0.9s','0.22s','0.9s','0.22s','0.9s','1.1s','0.9s','0.22s','0.9s','0.22s','0.9s','1.1s','0.9s','0.22s','0.9s','0.22s','0.9s','1.1s','0.9s','0.22s','0.9s','0.22s','0.9s','1.1s','0.9s','0.22s','0.9s','0.22s','0.9s']

347:         }
348:       },
349:       webpush: {
350:         headers: {
351:           Urgency: 'high',
352:           TTL: '600'
353:         },
354:         fcm_options: {
355:           link: JAYUMINTON_PUSH_CONFIG.appUrl
356:         }
357:       }
358:     }
359:   };
360: 
361:   return {
362:     url: JAYUMINTON_PUSH_CONFIG.fcmUrl,
363:     method: 'post',
364:     contentType: 'application/json; charset=utf-8',
365:     headers: {
366:       Authorization: 'Bearer ' + accessToken
367:     },
368:     payload: JSON.stringify(payload),
369:     muteHttpExceptions: true
370:   };
371: }

373: function cleanEvent_(body) {
374:   const type = String(body && body.type || 'court_assignment').trim();
375:   const assignmentId = String(body && body.assignmentId || '').trim();
376:   const sourceMembers = Array.isArray(body && body.members)
377:     ? body.members
378:     : [];
379: 
380:   if (!/^[A-Za-z0-9_.-]{8,500}$/.test(assignmentId)) {
381:     throw new Error('Invalid assignmentId.');
382:   }
383:   if (type !== 'court_assignment' && type !== 'wait1_ready') {
384:     throw new Error('Invalid notification type.');
385:   }
386: 
387:   const uniqueMembers = {};
388:   sourceMembers.forEach(function(member) {
389:     const id = cleanText_(member && member.id, 200);
390:     const name = cleanText_(member && member.name, 80);
391:     if (id && name) {
392:       uniqueMembers[id] = {id: id, name: name};
393:     }
394:   });
395:   const members = Object.keys(uniqueMembers).map(function(id) {
396:     return uniqueMembers[id];
397:   });
398: 
399:   if (type === 'court_assignment') {
400:     const courtNo = Number(body.courtNo);
401:     if ([1, 2, 3, 4].indexOf(courtNo) === -1 ||
402:         members.length !== 4) {
403:       throw new Error('Court assignment requires one valid court and four members.');
404:     }
405:     return {
406:       type: type,
407:       assignmentId: assignmentId,
408:       courtNo: courtNo,
409:       expectedCourtNo: 0,
410:       members: members
411:     };
412:   }
413: 
414:   const expectedCourtNo = Number(body.expectedCourtNo);
415:   if ([1, 2, 3, 4].indexOf(expectedCourtNo) === -1 ||
416:       members.length < 1 || members.length > 4) {
417:     throw new Error('Wait-1 notification requires one expected court and one to four members.');
418:   }
419:   return {
420:     type: type,
421:     assignmentId: assignmentId,
422:     courtNo: 0,
423:     expectedCourtNo: expectedCourtNo,
---
369:     muteHttpExceptions: true
370:   };
371: }

460: function limitWebPushTokens_(records) {
461:   const sorted = (records || []).slice().sort(function(left, right) {
462:     return Number(right.updatedAt || 0) - Number(left.updatedAt || 0);
463:   });
464:   const perMember = {};
465:   const result = [];
466: 
467:   sorted.forEach(function(record) {
468:     if (result.length >= JAYUMINTON_PUSH_CONFIG.maxTotalTokens) return;
469:     const memberId = String(record.memberId || '');
470:     perMember[memberId] = Number(perMember[memberId] || 0);
471:     if (perMember[memberId] >=
472:         JAYUMINTON_PUSH_CONFIG.maxTokensPerMember) return;
473:     perMember[memberId] += 1;
474:     result.push(record);
475:   });
476:   return result;
477: }

460: function limitWebPushTokens_(records) {
461:   const sorted = (records || []).slice().sort(function(left, right) {
462:     return Number(right.updatedAt || 0) - Number(left.updatedAt || 0);
463:   });
464:   const perMember = {};
465:   const result = [];
466: 
467:   sorted.forEach(function(record) {
468:     if (result.length >= JAYUMINTON_PUSH_CONFIG.maxTotalTokens) return;
469:     const memberId = String(record.memberId || '');
470:     perMember[memberId] = Number(perMember[memberId] || 0);
471:     if (perMember[memberId] >=
472:         JAYUMINTON_PUSH_CONFIG.maxTokensPerMember) return;
473:     perMember[memberId] += 1;
474:     result.push(record);
475:   });
476:   return result;
477: }

513: function getFcmAccessToken_(forceRefresh) {
514:   const cache = CacheService.getScriptCache();

524:   }

528:   }

533:     iss: credentials.client_email,
534:     scope: JAYUMINTON_PUSH_CONFIG.tokenScope,
535:     aud: JAYUMINTON_PUSH_CONFIG.tokenUrl,
536:     iat: now,
537:     exp: now + 3600
538:   }));
539:   const unsignedJwt = header + '.' + claim;
540:   const signature = Utilities.computeRsaSha256Signature(
541:     unsignedJwt,
542:     credentials.private_key
543:   );
544:   const assertion = unsignedJwt + '.' + base64UrlBytes_(signature);
545:

547:     method: 'post',
548:     payload: {
---

472:         JAYUMINTON_PUSH_CONFIG.maxTokensPerMember) return;

557:   if (status < 200 || status >= 300) {
558:     throw new Error('OAuth token request failed (' + status + '): ' + text);
559:   }
560: 
561:   const tokenResponse = JSON.parse(text);
562:   if (!tokenResponse.access_token) {
563:     throw new Error('OAuth response did not contain access_token.');
564:   }
565: 
566:   const expiresIn = Math.max(
567:     60,
568:     Number(tokenResponse.expires_in || 3600) - 300
569:   );
570:   cache.put(
571:     JAYUMINTON_PUSH_CONFIG.tokenCacheKey,
572:     tokenResponse.access_token,
573:     Math.min(21600, expiresIn)
574:   );
575:   return tokenResponse.access_token;
576: }

578: function base64Url_(text) {
579:   return base64UrlBytes_(Utilities.newBlob(text).getBytes());
580: }
581: 
582: function base64UrlBytes_(bytes) {
583:   return Utilities.base64EncodeWebSafe(bytes).replace(/=+$/g, '');
584: }

586: function secureEquals_(left, right) {
587:   if (!left || !right || left.length !== right.length) return false;
588:   let difference = 0;
589:   for (let index = 0; index < left.length; index += 1) {
590:     difference |= left.charCodeAt(index) ^ right.charCodeAt(index);
591:   }
592:   return difference === 0;
593: }

212: function withDocumentLock_(actionName, callback) {
213:   const lock = LockService.getDocumentLock();
214: 
215:   if (!lock.tryLock(15000)) {
216:     throw new Error(
217:       '다른 작업을 처리 중입니다. 잠시 후 다시 눌러주세요.'
218:     );

222:     ensureSetup_();

227:       logAction_(
228:         actionName,
229:         '정상 처리'
230:       );
231:     }
232: 
233:     return result;
234:   } catch (error) {
235:     logAction_(
236:       actionName,
237:       '오류: ' + String(error.message || error)
238:     );
239: 
240:     throw error;
241:   } finally {
242:     lock.releaseLock();
243:   }
244: }
